build, -RX7900XT, VICTORY_PORT_DESCRIPTORS=1 with the full fallback (VICTORY_PORT_DESCRIPTORS_EVERY=1,

records no store told of.

name, the title's id, name, files and build hash, and console names are replaced by <...> placeholders.
